  void _showTimeMachineModal() {
    HapticFeedback.mediumImpact();
    final store = globalMasterState.eventStore;
    final now = DateTime.now();
    final threeDaysAgoProfile = store.replayStateAt(now.subtract(const Duration(days: 3)));
    final sevenDaysAgoProfile = store.replayStateAt(now.subtract(const Duration(days: 7)));

    showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
      builder: (context) => DraggableScrollableSheet(
        initialChildSize: 0.75,
        minChildSize: 0.4,
        maxChildSize: 0.95,
        expand: false,
        builder: (context, scrollCtrl) {
          return _GlassPanel(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20.0),
            child: ListView(
              controller: scrollCtrl,
              children: [
                Row(
                  mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                  children: [
                    Row(
                      children: const [
                        Icon(Icons.history_toggle_off_rounded, color: AppColors.amber400),
                        SizedBox(width: 8),
                        Text('⏳ 學習時光機', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 17, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: AppColors.slate50)),
                      ],
                    ),
                    Container(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 4),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: AppColors.amber400.withOpacity(0.15),
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4),
                      ),
                      child: Text(
                        '${store.totalEventCount} 筆事件',
                        style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.amber400, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 11),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
                const Divider(color: Colors.white24, height: 24),
                const Text('時光倒流戰力回溯：', style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate400, fontSize: 13, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                const SizedBox(height: 10),
                Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(color: Colors.black45, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)),
                  child: Column(
                    children: [
                      _buildDiagRow('當下即時實力', '已斬殺 ${globalMasterState.masteredCount} 題 ｜ 錯題 ${globalMasterState.wrongCount} 題'),
                      const Divider(color: Colors.white12, height: 16),
                      _buildDiagRow('3 天前歷史戰力', '已斬殺 ${threeDaysAgoProfile.masteredIds.length} 題 ｜ 錯題 ${threeDaysAgoProfile.wrongQueueIds.length} 題'),
                      const Divider(color: Colors.white12, height: 16),
                      _buildDiagRow('7 天前歷史戰力', '已斬殺 ${sevenDaysAgoProfile.masteredIds.length} 題 ｜ 錯題 ${sevenDaysAgoProfile.wrongQueueIds.length} 題'),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 16),
                const Text('📜 最近 5 筆不可變作戰日誌:', style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate400, fontSize: 12)),
                const SizedBox(height: 8),
                Container(
                  height: 150,
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: Colors.black,
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                    border: Border.all(color: AppColors.slate800),
                  ),
                  child: store.totalEventCount == 0
                      ? const Center(child: Text('今日尚未發射作戰事件', style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate400, fontSize: 12)))
                      : ListView.builder(
                          itemCount: store.eventStream.length > 5 ? 5 : store.eventStream.length,
                          reverse: true,
                          itemBuilder: (context, i) {
                            final event = store.eventStream[store.eventStream.length - 1 - i];
                            return Padding(
                              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 2.0),
                              child: Text(
                                "> [${event.type.label}] ${event.payload} (${event.timestamp.hour}:${event.timestamp.minute.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}:${event.timestamp.second.toString().padLeft(2, '0')})",
                                style: const TextStyle(fontFamily: 'monospace', fontSize: 11, color: AppColors.amber400),
                              ),
                            );
                          },
                        ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 20),
                ElevatedButton.icon(
                  onPressed: () {
                    final dump = store.exportEventLogJson();
                    Clipboard.setData(ClipboardData(text: dump));
                    HapticFeedback.mediumImpact();
                    Navigator.pop(context);
                    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                      const SnackBar(
                        content: Text('📋 完整不可變事件日誌已複製至剪貼簿！'),
                        backgroundColor: AppColors.emerald400,
                      ),
                    );
                  },
                  icon: const Icon(Icons.archive_rounded, size: 16),
                  label: const Text('匯出完整事件流日誌'),
                  style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                    backgroundColor: AppColors.slate800,
                    foregroundColor: AppColors.slate50,
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          );
        },
      ),
    );
  }

  void _executeJsonImport() {
    try {
      final String rawJson = _jsonInputCtrl.text.trim();
      if (rawJson.isEmpty) return;
      final List<dynamic> parsedList = jsonDecode(rawJson);
      final List<QuestionItem> newQuestions = parsedList.map((json) => QuestionItem.fromJson(json)).toList();
      globalMasterState.adminImportMassiveQuestionsAsync(newQuestions);
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(
          content: Text('✅ 成功擴充 ${newQuestions.length} 題！目前總題數：${globalMasterState.totalQuestions}'),
          backgroundColor: AppColors.emerald400,
        ),
      );
      _jsonInputCtrl.clear();
      Navigator.of(context).pop();
    } catch (e) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(
          content: Text('❌ JSON 格式解析失敗: $e'),
          backgroundColor: AppColors.rose500,
        ),
      );
    }
  }

  void _showAdminImportModal() {
    showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
      builder: (context) {
        return _GlassPanel(
          padding: EdgeInsets.only(
            bottom: MediaQuery.of(context).viewInsets.bottom + 24,
            left: 16,
            right: 16,
            top: 24,
          ),
          child: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              const Text('⚙️ 題庫 JSON 擴充工具', style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.amber400, fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
              const SizedBox(height: 16),
              TextField(
                controller: _jsonInputCtrl,
                maxLines: 8,
                style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate50, fontSize: 12, fontFamily: 'monospace'),
                decoration: const InputDecoration(
                  hintText: '貼上 JSON Array 格式題庫...',
                  hintStyle: TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate400),
                  filled: true,
                  fillColor: Colors.black45,
                  border: OutlineInputBorder(),
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 16),
              ElevatedButton(
                onPressed: _executeJsonImport,
                style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: AppColors.emerald400),
                child: const Text('寫入本地資料庫 (Overwrite)', style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate950)),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }

  void _handleExportCheatSheet() {
    if (!globalMasterState.isPro) {
      HapticFeedback.heavyImpact();
      _showProRequiredModal();
      return;
    }
    HapticFeedback.mediumImpact();
    final wrongList = globalMasterState.allQuestions.where((q) => globalMasterState.profile.wrongQueueIds.contains(q.id)).toList();
    final htmlCode = CheatSheetGenerator.buildHtmlDocument(wrongQuestions: wrongList, licenseType: "00700 乙級室內配線");
    Clipboard.setData(ClipboardData(text: htmlCode));
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
      const SnackBar(
        content: Text('📋 考前小抄 HTML 代碼已成功複製至剪貼簿！可貼至記事本存為 .html 列印'),
        backgroundColor: AppColors.emerald400,
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildSpecTicker() {
    return _GlassPanel(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 12),
      borderColor: AppColors.amber400.withOpacity(0.3),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          AnimatedBuilder(
            animation: _pulseAnimation,
            builder: (context, child) => Opacity(
              opacity: _pulseAnimation.value,
              child: const Icon(Icons.verified_rounded, color: AppColors.amber400, size: 20),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 12),
          Expanded(
            child: AnimatedSwitcher(
              duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 400),
              transitionBuilder: (child, anim) => FadeTransition(
                opacity: anim,
                child: SlideTransition(
                  position: Tween<Offset>(begin: const Offset(0, 0.5), end: Offset.zero).animate(anim),
                  child: child,
                ),
              ),
              child: Text(
                _technicalSpecList[_currentSpecIndex],
                key: ValueKey<int>(_currentSpecIndex),
                style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate50, fontSize: 13, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                maxLines: 1,
                overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  // --- FSRS 今日待消滅 X 題 主行動卡片 ---

      shadowColor: hasDue ? const Color(0xFF9F1239) : Colors.black,

          context,
          MaterialPageRoute(builder: (context) => const ElectroQuizArenaView()),
        );
      },
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
        child: Row(
          children: [
            Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: hasDue ? Colors.white24 : AppColors.slate700,
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
              ),
              child: Icon(
                hasDue ? Icons.local_fire_department_rounded : Icons.check_circle_outline_rounded,

                  ),

                    hasDue ? '點擊一鍵發動艾賓浩斯記憶消除戰' : '無任何到達遺忘臨界點之考題，繼續保持！',

                            )

                        ),
                      );
                    }).toList(),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),

      },
    );
  }

=== lib/screens/exam_arena_screen.dart ===

import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:ui';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import '../theme/app_theme.dart';
import '../models/core_types.dart';
import '../main.dart';

enum ExamPhase { lobby, active, result }

class _DataPanel extends StatelessWidget {
  final Widget child;
  final EdgeInsetsGeometry padding;
  final Color borderColor;
  const _DataPanel({required this.child, this.padding = const EdgeInsets.all(16), this.borderColor = Colors.white12});
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return ClipRRect(
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
      child: BackdropFilter(
        filter: ImageFilter.blur(sigmaX: 8.0, sigmaY: 8.0),
        child: Container(
          padding: padding,
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: AppColors.slate900.withOpacity(0.8),
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
            border: Border.all(color: borderColor, width: 1.5),
          ),
          child: child,
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _SolidButton extends StatelessWidget {
  final String text; final Color color; final VoidCallback onPressed; final IconData? icon;
  const _SolidButton({required this.text, required this.color, required this.onPressed, this.icon});
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return ElevatedButton(
      onPressed: () { HapticFeedback.mediumImpact(); onPressed(); },
      style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: color, foregroundColor: AppColors.slate950, elevation: 0, shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10)), padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16)),
      child: Row(mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center, children: [if (icon != null) ...[Icon(icon, size: 20), const SizedBox(width: 8)], Text(text, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, letterSpacing: 0.5))]),
    );
  }
}

class _DevOpsFourKeysDashboard extends StatelessWidget {
  final double failureRate; final int avgSecondsPerQuestion;
  const _DevOpsFourKeysDashboard({required this.failureRate, required this.avgSecondsPerQuestion});
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return _DataPanel(
      borderColor: AppColors.blue500.withOpacity(0.5),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween, children: const [Row(children: [Icon(Icons.speed_rounded, color: AppColors.blue500, size: 20), SizedBox(width: 8), Text('DevOps DORA 四大作戰指標', style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate50, fontSize: 15, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold))]), Text('DORA Metrics', style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.blue500, fontSize: 10, fontFamily: 'monospace', fontWeight: FontWeight.bold))]),
          const SizedBox(height: 16),
          Row(children: [Expanded(child: _buildMetricCard('變更失敗率 (錯題率)', '${failureRate.toStringAsFixed(1)}%', failureRate < 25 ? AppColors.emerald400 : AppColors.rose500)), const SizedBox(width: 10), Expanded(child: _buildMetricCard('平均前置時間 (解題)', '${avgSecondsPerQuestion}s / 題', avgSecondsPerQuestion < 45 ? AppColors.emerald400 : AppColors.amber400))]),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
  Widget _buildMetricCard(String title, String value, Color color) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(color: Colors.black45, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8), border: Border.all(color: color.withOpacity(0.3))),
      child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [Text(title, style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate400, fontSize: 10, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)), const SizedBox(height: 6), Text(value, style: TextStyle(color: color, fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, fontFamily: 'monospace'))]),
    );
  }
}

class MockExamArenaView extends StatefulWidget {
  const MockExamArenaView({super.key});
  @override
  State<MockExamArenaView> createState() => _MockExamArenaViewState();
}

class _MockExamArenaViewState extends State<MockExamArenaView> {
  ExamPhase _phase = ExamPhase.lobby;
  List<QuestionItem> _examQuestions = [];
  final Map<int, List<int>> _answers = {};
  final Set<int> _flaggedIndices = {};
  Timer? _timer;
  int _secondsRemaining = 6000;
  int _currentIndex = 0;
  int _singleScore = 0;
  int _multiScore = 0;
  int _totalScore = 0;
  List<QuestionItem> _wrongQuestions = [];

  @override
  void dispose() { _timer?.cancel(); super.dispose(); }

  void _startExam() {
    HapticFeedback.heavyImpact();
    final all = globalMasterState.allQuestions;
    final singles = all.where((q) => q.type == 'single').toList()..shuffle();
    final multiples = all.where((q) => q.type == 'multiple').toList()..shuffle();
    final paper = <QuestionItem>[];
    paper.addAll(singles.take(60)); paper.addAll(multiples.take(20));
    setState(() { _examQuestions = paper; _answers.clear(); _flaggedIndices.clear(); _secondsRemaining = 6000; _currentIndex = 0; _phase = ExamPhase.active; _wrongQuestions.clear(); });
    _timer?.cancel();
    _timer = Timer.periodic(const Duration(seconds: 1), (timer) {
      if (!mounted) { timer.cancel(); return; }
      if (_secondsRemaining > 0) setState(() => _secondsRemaining--); else _finishExam(isTimeOut: true);
    });
  }

  void _finishExam({bool isTimeOut = false}) {
    _timer?.cancel(); HapticFeedback.heavyImpact();
    int singlePts = 0; int multiPts = 0; final wrongs = <QuestionItem>[];
    for (int i = 0; i < _examQuestions.length; i++) {
      final q = _examQuestions[i]; final userSelected = _answers[i] ?? []; final officialAns = q.answer;
      bool passed = false;
      if (q.type == 'single') { if (userSelected.isNotEmpty && officialAns.contains(userSelected.first)) { passed = true; singlePts += 1; } }
      else { final uSet = userSelected.toSet(); final oSet = officialAns.toSet(); if (uSet.length == oSet.length && uSet.containsAll(oSet)) { passed = true; multiPts += 2; } }
      if (!passed) wrongs.add(q);
    }
    setState(() { _singleScore = singlePts; _multiScore = multiPts; _totalScore = singlePts + multiPts; _wrongQuestions = wrongs; _phase = ExamPhase.result; });
    if (isTimeOut && mounted) ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(const SnackBar(content: Text('⏰ 時間已到！系統已自動封存閱卷。'), backgroundColor: AppColors.rose500));
  }

  void _onOptionTapped(int optionIndex) {
    HapticFeedback.selectionClick();
    final q = _examQuestions[_currentIndex]; final optionNumber = optionIndex + 1; final currentSelected = List<int>.from(_answers[_currentIndex] ?? []);
    if (q.type == 'single') {
      _answers[_currentIndex] = [optionNumber];
      if (_currentIndex < _examQuestions.length - 1) { Future.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 300), () { if (mounted && _phase == ExamPhase.active) setState(() => _currentIndex++); }); }
    } else {
      if (currentSelected.contains(optionNumber)) currentSelected.remove(optionNumber); else currentSelected.add(optionNumber);
      if (currentSelected.isEmpty) _answers.remove(_currentIndex); else _answers[_currentIndex] = currentSelected;
    }
    setState(() {});
  }

  void _showSubmitConfirmationDialog() {
    HapticFeedback.mediumImpact();
    final unanswered = _examQuestions.length - _answers.keys.length;
    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) {
        return AlertDialog(
          backgroundColor: AppColors.slate900,
          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12), side: const BorderSide(color: AppColors.amber400, width: 2)),
          title: const Text('⚠️ 確認封存交卷？', style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate50, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
          content: Column(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [Text('剩餘時間：${_formatTime(_secondsRemaining)}', style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.amber400, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 16)), const SizedBox(height: 12), Text(unanswered > 0 ? '警告：您還有 $unanswered 題尚未填答！' : '系統檢測：80 題已全數填答完畢。', style: TextStyle(color: unanswered > 0 ? AppColors.rose500 : AppColors.emerald400, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)), const SizedBox(height: 8), const Text('交卷後將無法修改答案，並立即進入資料分析程序。', style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate400, fontSize: 12))]),
          actions: [TextButton(onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context), child: const Text('返回作答', style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate400))), ElevatedButton(onPressed: () { Navigator.pop(context); _finishExam(); }, style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: AppColors.amber400, foregroundColor: AppColors.slate950), child: const Text('確認交卷', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)))],
        );
      },
    );
  }

  void _showAnswerSheetModal() {
    HapticFeedback.lightImpact();
    showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context, isScrollControlled: true, backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
      builder: (context) {
        return DraggableScrollableSheet(
          initialChildSize: 0.85, minChildSize: 0.5, maxChildSize: 0.95, expand: false,
          builder: (context, scrollCtrl) {
            return _DataPanel(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 8),
              child: Column(
                children: [
                  Container(width: 40, height: 5, margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 8), decoration: BoxDecoration(color: AppColors.slate700, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4))),
                  Row(mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween, children: [const Text('📊 全景狀態矩陣 (答題卡)', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: AppColors.slate50)), Text('完成度: ${_answers.length}/${_examQuestions.length}', style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.emerald400, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, fontFamily: 'monospace'))]),
                  const Divider(color: AppColors.slate800, height: 24),
                  Row(mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceEvenly, children: [_buildLegendDot(AppColors.emerald400, '已答'), _buildLegendDot(AppColors.rose500, '標記疑問'), _buildLegendDot(AppColors.slate800, '未作答')]),
                  const SizedBox(height: 16),
                  Expanded(
                    child: GridView.builder(
                      controller: scrollCtrl, physics: const BouncingScrollPhysics(), gridDelegate: const SliverGridDelegateWithFixedCrossAxisCount(crossAxisCount: 7, crossAxisSpacing: 8, mainAxisSpacing: 8),
                      itemCount: _examQuestions.length,
                      itemBuilder: (context, i) {
                        final isAnswered = _answers.containsKey(i); final isFlagged = _flaggedIndices.contains(i); final isCurrent = i == _currentIndex;
                        Color tileBg = AppColors.slate800; Color textCol = AppColors.slate400; Color borderCol = Colors.transparent;
                        if (isFlagged) { tileBg = AppColors.rose500.withOpacity(0.2); textCol = AppColors.rose500; borderCol = AppColors.rose500; }
                        else if (isAnswered) { tileBg = AppColors.emerald400.withOpacity(0.2); textCol = AppColors.emerald400; }
                        if (isCurrent) borderCol = AppColors.amber400;
                        return GestureDetector(onTap: () { HapticFeedback.selectionClick(); setState(() => _currentIndex = i); Navigator.pop(context); }, child: Container(decoration: BoxDecoration(color: tileBg, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6), border: Border.all(color: borderCol, width: isCurrent ? 2.5 : 1)), alignment: Alignment.center, child: Text('${i + 1}', style: TextStyle(color: textCol, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 13))));
                      },
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            );
          },
        );
      },
    );
  }

  Widget _buildLegendDot(Color color, String label) {
    return Row(children: [Container(width: 10, height: 10, decoration: BoxDecoration(color: color, shape: BoxShape.circle)), const SizedBox(width: 4), Text(label, style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate400, fontSize: 12))]);
  }
  String _formatTime(int totalSeconds) { final m = (totalSeconds ~/ 60).toString().padLeft(2, '0'); final s = (totalSeconds % 60).toString().padLeft(2, '0'); return '$m:$s'; }

  Widget _buildLobbyView() {
    return SingleChildScrollView(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24.0),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          _DataPanel(borderColor: AppColors.amber400, padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 32, horizontal: 16), child: Column(children: const [Icon(Icons.gavel_rounded, size: 56, color: AppColors.amber400), SizedBox(height: 16), Text('勞動部 00700 乙級學科', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 14, color: AppColors.slate400, letterSpacing: 2.0)), SizedBox(height: 4), Text('全真模擬考場', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 28, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, color: AppColors.slate50))])),
          const SizedBox(height: 24), const Text('⚖️ 國家考試規範配置：', style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate400, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 13)), const SizedBox(height: 12),
          Container(padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16), decoration: BoxDecoration(color: AppColors.slate900, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12), border: Border.all(color: AppColors.slate800)), child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: const [Text('• 總題數：80 題隨機抽取', style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate50, fontSize: 14, height: 1.6)), Text('• 題型分佈：單選 60 題 (每題 1 分) + 複選 20 題 (每題 2 分)', style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate50, fontSize: 14, height: 1.6)), Text('• 考試時間：100 分鐘 (時間到強制收卷)', style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate50, fontSize: 14, height: 1.6)), Text('• 合格標準：總分達 60 分', style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.emerald400, fontSize: 14, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, height: 1.6)), Text('• 注意事項：複選題全對才給分，答錯不倒扣。', style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.rose500, fontSize: 14, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, height: 1.6))])),
          const SizedBox(height: 32), _SolidButton(text: '系統就緒，立即進入考場', color: AppColors.amber400, icon: Icons.play_arrow_rounded, onPressed: _startExam),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildActiveExamView() {
    final q = _examQuestions[_currentIndex]; final userSelected = _answers[_currentIndex] ?? []; final isFlagged = _flaggedIndices.contains(_currentIndex); final isUrgent = _secondsRemaining < 600;
    return Column(
      children: [
        Container(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16, vertical: 12), decoration: const BoxDecoration(color: AppColors.slate950, border: Border(bottom: BorderSide(color: AppColors.slate800, width: 2))),
          child: Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              Row(children: [Icon(Icons.timer_outlined, color: isUrgent ? AppColors.rose500 : AppColors.amber400, size: 22), const SizedBox(width: 8), Text(_formatTime(_secondsRemaining), style: TextStyle(fontFamily: 'monospace', fontSize: 20, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, color: isUrgent ? AppColors.rose500 : AppColors.amber400, letterSpacing: 1.0))]),
              Row(children: [IconButton(icon: Icon(isFlagged ? Icons.flag_rounded : Icons.outlined_flag_rounded, color: isFlagged ? AppColors.rose500 : AppColors.slate400, size: 26), onPressed: () { HapticFeedback.lightImpact(); setState(() { isFlagged ? _flaggedIndices.remove(_currentIndex) : _flaggedIndices.add(_currentIndex); }); }), const SizedBox(width: 4), IconButton(icon: const Icon(Icons.grid_view_rounded, color: AppColors.slate300, size: 26), onPressed: _showAnswerSheetModal)]),
            ],
          ),
        ),
        LinearProgressIndicator(value: (_currentIndex + 1) / _examQuestions.length, backgroundColor: AppColors.slate900, valueColor: const AlwaysStoppedAnimation(AppColors.blue500), minHeight: 3),
        Expanded(
          child: SingleChildScrollView(
            physics: const BouncingScrollPhysics(), padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Row(mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween, children: [Container(padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 4), decoration: BoxDecoration(color: q.type == 'single' ? AppColors.blue500 : AppColors.rose500, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6)), child: Text(q.type == 'single' ? '單選 (1分)' : '⚠️ 複選 (2分)', style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 13, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold))), Text('#${_currentIndex + 1} / 80', style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate400, fontFamily: 'monospace', fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 14))]),
                const SizedBox(height: 24), Text(q.question, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 18, color: AppColors.slate50, height: 1.6, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600)), const SizedBox(height: 32),
                ...List.generate(q.options.length, (optIdx) {
                  final optNum = optIdx + 1; final isSelected = userSelected.contains(optNum);
                  return GestureDetector(
                    onTap: () => _onOptionTapped(optIdx),
                    child: AnimatedContainer(
                      duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 150), margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12), padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(color: isSelected ? AppColors.amber400.withOpacity(0.15) : AppColors.slate900, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12), border: Border.all(color: isSelected ? AppColors.amber400 : AppColors.slate800, width: isSelected ? 2 : 1)),
                      child: Row(children: [Container(width: 28, height: 28, alignment: Alignment.center, decoration: BoxDecoration(shape: BoxShape.circle, color: isSelected ? AppColors.amber400 : AppColors.slate800), child: Text('$optNum', style: TextStyle(color: isSelected ? AppColors.slate950 : AppColors.slate400, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold))), const SizedBox(width: 16), Expanded(child: Text(q.options[optIdx], style: TextStyle(color: isSelected ? AppColors.amber400 : AppColors.slate50, fontSize: 16)))]),
                    ),
                  );
                }),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
        Container(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16), decoration: const BoxDecoration(color: AppColors.slate950, border: Border(top: BorderSide(color: AppColors.slate800))),
          child: Row(
            children: [
              Expanded(flex: 2, child: OutlinedButton(onPressed: _currentIndex > 0 ? () => setState(() => _currentIndex--) : null, style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(side: const BorderSide(color: AppColors.slate700), padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16)), child: const Text('上一題', style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate300)))),
              const SizedBox(width: 12),
              Expanded(flex: 3, child: _currentIndex < _examQuestions.length - 1 ? _SolidButton(text: '下一題', color: AppColors.amber400, onPressed: () => setState(() => _currentIndex++)) : _SolidButton(text: '交卷', color: AppColors.rose500, icon: Icons.outbox_rounded, onPressed: _showSubmitConfirmationDialog)),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildResultView() {
    final isPassed = _totalScore >= 60;
    final failureRate = (_wrongQuestions.length / 80) * 100;
    final elapsedSeconds = 6000 - _secondsRemaining;
    final avgSecPerQ = elapsedSeconds ~/ 80;

    return SingleChildScrollView(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20.0),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          _DataPanel(
            borderColor: isPassed ? AppColors.emerald400 : AppColors.rose500, padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
            child: Column(
              children: [
                Icon(isPassed ? Icons.verified_rounded : Icons.dangerous_rounded, size: 64, color: isPassed ? AppColors.emerald400 : AppColors.rose500), const SizedBox(height: 12), Text(isPassed ? '判定結果：合格' : '判定結果：不合格', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 20, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: isPassed ? AppColors.emerald400 : AppColors.rose500)), const SizedBox(height: 16), Text('$_totalScore', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 64, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900, color: isPassed ? AppColors.emerald400 : AppColors.rose500, fontFamily: 'monospace')), const Text('最終測驗總分 (滿分 100)', style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate400, fontSize: 13)), const Divider(color: AppColors.slate800, height: 32),
                Row(
                  mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceAround, // ✅ 已修復 MainAxisAlignment 拼寫錯誤
                  children: [
                    Column(children: [Text('$_singleScore / 60', style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 20, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: AppColors.slate50)), const SizedBox(height: 4), const Text('單選題得分', style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate400, fontSize: 12))]),
                    Container(height: 40, width: 1, color: AppColors.slate700),
                    Column(children: [Text('$_multiScore / 40', style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 20, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: AppColors.slate50)), const SizedBox(height: 4), const Text('複選題得分', style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate400, fontSize: 12))]),
                  ],
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 20),
          _DevOpsFourKeysDashboard(failureRate: failureRate, avgSecondsPerQuestion: avgSecPerQ),
          const SizedBox(height: 20),
          if (_wrongQuestions.isNotEmpty) ...[
            _DataPanel(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Row(children: const [Icon(Icons.analytics_rounded, color: AppColors.amber400, size: 20), SizedBox(width: 8), Text('CIO 戰損資料復盤', style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate50, fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold))]), const SizedBox(height: 12), Text('本次測驗共產生 ${_wrongQuestions.length} 道錯誤判斷 (包含未答或未完全答對之複選題)。', style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate400, fontSize: 13, height: 1.5)), const SizedBox(height: 16),
                  _SolidButton(
                    text: '封裝錯題並寫入 FSRS 資料庫', color: AppColors.amber400, icon: Icons.memory_rounded,
                    onPressed: () {
                      for (final q in _wrongQuestions) globalMasterState.recordWrongAnswer(q.id);
                      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('✅ 成功將 ${_wrongQuestions.length} 題寫入急診佇列！'), backgroundColor: AppColors.emerald400));
                      setState(() { _wrongQuestions.clear(); });
                    },
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
          ],
          OutlinedButton.icon(
            onPressed: () { HapticFeedback.selectionClick(); setState(() => _phase = ExamPhase.lobby); },
            icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh_rounded, color: AppColors.slate50), label: const Text('返回大廳重啟測驗', style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate50)),
            style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(side: const BorderSide(color: AppColors.slate700), padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16)),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: AppColors.slate950,
      appBar: AppBar(title: const Text('🏛️ 全真模擬考場', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.w900)), elevation: 0),
      body: SafeArea(child: _phase == ExamPhase.active ? _buildActiveExamView() : (_phase == ExamPhase.result ? _buildResultView() : _buildLobbyView())),
    );
  }
}

// 專案名稱：電光證照霸 PRO (ElectroSolar Pass)
// 檔案：lib/screens/paywall_screen.dart

import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:ui';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';

import '../main.dart';

/// 唯一官方正式產品 ID (Non-Consumable 非消耗型終身買斷)
const String kProLifetimeProductId = 'esp_pro_lifetime_590';
const Set<String> _kProductIds = {kProLifetimeProductId};

class _IosGlassCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final Widget child;
  final EdgeInsetsGeometry padding;
  final Color borderColor;

  const _IosGlassCard({
    required this.child,
    this.padding = const EdgeInsets.all(20),
    this.borderColor = Colors.white12,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return ClipRRect(
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
      child: BackdropFilter(
        filter: ImageFilter.blur(sigmaX: 20.0, sigmaY: 20.0),
        child: Container(
          padding: padding,
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: AppColors.slate900.withOpacity(0.75),
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
            border: Border.all(color: borderColor, width: 1.5),
          ),
          child: child,
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _AppleStylePayButton extends StatefulWidget {
  final String label;
  final String priceText;
  final VoidCallback? onPressed;
  final bool isLoading;

  const _AppleStylePayButton({
    required this.label,
    required this.priceText,
    required this.onPressed,
    this.isLoading = false,
  });

  @override
  State<_AppleStylePayButton> createState() => _AppleStylePayButtonState();
}

class _AppleStylePayButtonState extends State<_AppleStylePayButton> {
  bool _isPressed = false;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final bool isDisabled = widget.isLoading || widget.onPressed == null;
    return GestureDetector(
      onTapDown: (_) {
        if (!isDisabled) {
          HapticFeedback.lightImpact();
          setState(() => _isPressed = true);
        }
      },
      onTapUp: (_) {
        if (!isDisabled) {
          HapticFeedback.heavyImpact();
          setState(() => _isPressed = false);
          widget.onPressed!();
        }
      },
      onTapCancel: () => setState(() => _isPressed = false),
      child: AnimatedContainer(
        duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 120),
        curve: Curves.easeOut,
        height: 56,
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 20),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: _isPressed || isDisabled ? AppColors.amber400.withOpacity(0.5) : AppColors.amber400,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
          boxShadow: [
            BoxShadow(
              color: AppColors.amber400.withOpacity(_isPressed || isDisabled ? 0.0 : 0.4),
              blurRadius: _isPressed ? 4 : 12,
              offset: Offset(0, _isPressed ? 2 : 4),
            )
          ],
        ),
        child: Row(
          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
          children: [
            Row(
              children: [
                const Icon(Icons.apple_rounded, color: AppColors.slate950, size: 24),
                const SizedBox(width: 8),
                Text(
                  widget.isLoading ? '安全連線中...' : widget.label,
                  style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate950, fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            if (widget.isLoading)
              const SizedBox(
                width: 20,
                height: 20,
                child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: AppColors.slate950, strokeWidth: 2),
              )
            else
              Text(
                widget.priceText,
                style: const TextStyle(
                  color: AppColors.slate950,
                  fontSize: 18,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w900,
                  fontFamily: 'monospace',
                ),
              ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class HardcorePaywallView extends StatefulWidget {
  const HardcorePaywallView({super.key});

  @override
  State<HardcorePaywallView> createState() => _HardcorePaywallViewState();
}

class _HardcorePaywallViewState extends State<HardcorePaywallView> {
  final TextEditingController _licenseKeyCtrl = TextEditingController();
  String _activationFeedback = '';

  InAppPurchase? _iap;
  StreamSubscription<List<PurchaseDetails>>? _subscription;
  ProductDetails? _proProductDetails;
  bool _isIapAvailable = false;
  bool _isPurchasePending = false;
  bool _isRestoring = false;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    if (!kIsWeb) {
      _iap = InAppPurchase.instance;
      _initStoreInfo();
    }
  }

  Future<void> _initStoreInfo() async {
    if (_iap == null) return;

    final Stream<List<PurchaseDetails>> purchaseUpdated = _iap!.purchaseStream;
    _subscription = purchaseUpdated.listen(
      (purchaseDetailsList) {
        _listenToPurchaseUpdated(purchaseDetailsList);
      },
      onDone: () => _subscription?.cancel(),
      onError: (error) => debugPrint('IAP Stream Error: $error'),
    );

    final bool isAvailable = await _iap!.isAvailable();
    if (!isAvailable) {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _isIapAvailable = false);
      return;
    }

    final ProductDetailsResponse response = await _iap!.queryProductDetails(_kProductIds);
    if (response.error == null && response.productDetails.isNotEmpty) {
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          _isIapAvailable = true;
          _proProductDetails = response.productDetails.firstWhere(
            (p) => p.id == kProLifetimeProductId,
            orElse: () => response.productDetails.first,
          );
        });
      }
    }
  }

  void _listenToPurchaseUpdated(List<PurchaseDetails> purchaseDetailsList) {
    for (final PurchaseDetails purchaseDetails in purchaseDetailsList) {
      if (purchaseDetails.status == PurchaseStatus.pending) {
        if (mounted) setState(() => _isPurchasePending = true);
      } else {
        if (mounted) {
          setState(() {
            _isPurchasePending = false;
            _isRestoring = false;
          });
        }
        if (purchaseDetails.status == PurchaseStatus.error) {
          ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
            SnackBar(
              content: Text('❌ 交易失敗：${purchaseDetails.error?.message}'),
              backgroundColor: AppColors.rose500,
            ),
          );
        } else if (purchaseDetails.status == PurchaseStatus.purchased ||
            purchaseDetails.status == PurchaseStatus.restored) {
          globalMasterState.activateLicense('VIP888');
          ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
            SnackBar(
              content: Text(
                purchaseDetails.status == PurchaseStatus.restored
                    ? '🎉 成功恢復購買！PRO 終身旗艦權限已同步！'
                    : '💳 Apple Pay 付款成功！PRO 終身旗艦版已啟用！',
              ),
              backgroundColor: AppColors.emerald400,
            ),
          );
        }
        if (purchaseDetails.pendingCompletePurchase) {
          _iap?.completePurchase(purchaseDetails);
        }
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _subscription?.cancel();
    _licenseKeyCtrl.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _attemptUnlock({String? directCode}) {
    final code = directCode ?? _licenseKeyCtrl.text;
    final success = globalMasterState.activateLicense(code);
    if (success) {
      HapticFeedback.heavyImpact();
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(
          content: Text('🎉 恭喜！PRO 終身旗艦版已全功能永久啟用！'),
          backgroundColor: AppColors.emerald400,
        ),
      );
    } else {
      HapticFeedback.vibrate();
      setState(() => _activationFeedback = '❌ 授權碼無效，請輸入 VIP888 或 PASS2026');
    }
  }

  void _buyProduct() {
    if (kIsWeb || _iap == null || !_isIapAvailable || _proProductDetails == null) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(
          content: Text('💡 Web/展示模式：請輸入體驗碼 VIP888 解鎖。iOS 真機將直連 Apple Pay！'),
          backgroundColor: AppColors.amber400,
        ),
      );
      return;
    }
    _iap!.buyNonConsumable(purchaseParam: PurchaseParam(productDetails: _proProductDetails!));
  }

  Future<void> _restorePurchases() async {
    HapticFeedback.mediumImpact();
    if (kIsWeb || _iap == null || !_isIapAvailable) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(
          content: Text('💡 當前環境無需恢復，請輸入 VIP888 體驗完整功能。'),
          backgroundColor: AppColors.amber400,
        ),
      );
      return;
    }

    setState(() => _isRestoring = true);
    try {
      await _iap!.restorePurchases();
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() => _isRestoring = false);
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          SnackBar(
            content: Text('❌ 恢復購買請求失敗: $e'),
            backgroundColor: AppColors.rose500,
          ),
        );
      }
    }
  }

  void _openInstantCheckoutModal() {
    HapticFeedback.mediumImpact();
    showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
      builder: (context) {
        return _IosGlassCard(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
          child: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
            children: [
              Row(
                mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                children: [
                  const Text('⚡ Apple IAP 快速結帳通道', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: AppColors.amber400)),
                  IconButton(
                    icon: const Icon(Icons.close, color: AppColors.slate400, size: 20),
                    onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
              const Divider(color: Colors.white24, height: 20),
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(color: Colors.black45, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
                child: Column(
                  children: [
                    Row(
                      mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                      children: const [
                        Text('雙證照終身買斷旗艦包', style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate50, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600)),
                        Text('NT\$ 890', style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate400, decoration: TextDecoration.lineThrough, fontSize: 13)),
                      ],
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 6),
                    Row(
                      mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                      children: const [
                        Text('考前衝刺限時折扣', style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.rose500, fontSize: 12)),
                        Text('- NT\$ 300', style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.rose500, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                      ],
                    ),
                    const Divider(color: Colors.white12, height: 16),
                    Row(
                      mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                      children: const [

      context: context,

      builder: (context) => DraggableScrollableSheet(
        initialChildSize: 0.8,
        minChildSize: 0.5,
        maxChildSize: 0.95,
        builder: (context, scrollCtrl) => _LegalDocViewer(title: title, content: content),
      ),
    );
  }

=== lib/screens/placement_test_screen.dart ===

// ==========================================
// 專案名稱：電光證照霸 PRO (ElectroSolar Pass)
// 檔案：lib/screens/placement_test_screen.dart
// 說明：[10分鐘實力落點快測] 跨 8 大領域 15 題抽樣診斷與得分預測 (依賴補齊版)
// ==========================================

import 'dart:math' as math;
import 'dart:ui';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import '../theme/app_theme.dart';
import '../models/core_types.dart';
import '../models/skill_tree_domain.dart';
import '../utils/telemetry_logger.dart'; // 🚀 補齊 telemetry 遙測探針引用
import '../main.dart';

/// 實力快測診斷報告模型
class PlacementDiagnosticReport {
  final int totalQuestions;
  final int correctCount;
  final double predictedScore;
  final ElectricalDomain weakestDomain;
  final Map<ElectricalDomain, int> domainScores;

  const PlacementDiagnosticReport({
    required this.totalQuestions,
    required this.correctCount,
    required this.predictedScore,
    required this.weakestDomain,
    required this.domainScores,
  });

  bool get isPredictedPass => predictedScore >= 60.0;
}

class PlacementTestScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const PlacementTestScreen({super.key});

  @override
  State<PlacementTestScreen> createState() => _PlacementTestScreenState();
}

class _PlacementTestScreenState extends State<PlacementTestScreen> {
  List<QuestionItem> _paper = [];
  final Map<int, List<int>> _userAnswers = {};
  int _currentIndex = 0;
  bool _isFinished = false;
  PlacementDiagnosticReport? _report;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _generatePlacementPaper();
  }

  /// 跨 8 大領域精準抽樣 15 題（每個領域 1~2 題）
  void _generatePlacementPaper() {
    final allQuestions = globalMasterState.allQuestions;
    if (allQuestions.isEmpty) return;

    final random = math.Random();
    final List<QuestionItem> selected = [];

    // 1. 遍歷 8 大領域，各隨機抽取 1~2 題
    for (final domain in ElectricalDomain.values) {
      final domainQuestions = allQuestions.where(
        (q) => SkillTreeDomainMapper.resolveDomain(q) == domain
      ).toList();

      if (domainQuestions.isNotEmpty) {
        domainQuestions.shuffle(random);
        final takeCount = math.min(domainQuestions.length, 2);
        selected.addAll(domainQuestions.take(takeCount));
      }
    }

    // 2. 若總數不足 15 題，從全池隨機補足至 15 題
    if (selected.length < 15 && allQuestions.length >= 15) {
      final selectedIds = selected.map((q) => q.id).toSet();
      final remaining = allQuestions.where((q) => !selectedIds.contains(q.id)).toList()..shuffle(random);
      selected.addAll(remaining.take(15 - selected.length));
    }

    // 3. 切割為精準 15 題並打亂順序
    selected.shuffle(random);
    _paper = selected.take(15).toList();
  }

  void _onOptionTapped(int optionIndex) {
    HapticFeedback.selectionClick();
    final q = _paper[_currentIndex];
    final optionNumber = optionIndex + 1;
    final currentSelected = List<int>.from(_userAnswers[_currentIndex] ?? []);

    setState(() {
      if (q.type == 'single') {
        _userAnswers[_currentIndex] = [optionNumber];
      } else {
        if (currentSelected.contains(optionNumber)) {
          currentSelected.remove(optionNumber);
        } else {
          currentSelected.add(optionNumber);
        }
        if (currentSelected.isEmpty) {
          _userAnswers.remove(_currentIndex);
        } else {
          _userAnswers[_currentIndex] = currentSelected;
        }
      }
    });
  }

  void _submitAndCalculateReport() {
    HapticFeedback.heavyImpact();
    int correctCount = 0;
    final Map<ElectricalDomain, int> domainTotal = {};
    final Map<ElectricalDomain, int> domainCorrect = {};

    for (final domain in ElectricalDomain.values) {
      domainTotal[domain] = 0;
      domainCorrect[domain] = 0;
    }

    for (int i = 0; i < _paper.length; i++) {
      final q = _paper[i];
      final domain = SkillTreeDomainMapper.resolveDomain(q);
      domainTotal[domain] = (domainTotal[domain] ?? 0) + 1;

      final userSelected = _userAnswers[i] ?? [];
      final officialAns = q.answer;

      bool isCorrect = false;
      if (q.type == 'single') {
        if (userSelected.isNotEmpty && officialAns.contains(userSelected.first)) {
          isCorrect = true;
        }
      } else {
        final uSet = userSelected.toSet();
        final oSet = officialAns.toSet();
        if (uSet.length == oSet.length && uSet.containsAll(oSet)) {
          isCorrect = true;
        }
      }

      if (isCorrect) {
        correctCount++;
        domainCorrect[domain] = (domainCorrect[domain] ?? 0) + 1;
      } else {
        globalMasterState.recordWrongAnswer(q.id);
      }
    }

    final double predicted = (correctCount / _paper.length) * 100.0;

    // 判定最弱領域 (答對率最低者)
    ElectricalDomain weakest = ElectricalDomain.transformer;
    double minRate = 2.0;

    for (final domain in ElectricalDomain.values) {
      final tot = domainTotal[domain] ?? 0;
      if (tot > 0) {
        final rate = (domainCorrect[domain] ?? 0) / tot;
        if (rate < minRate) {
          minRate = rate;
          weakest = domain;
        }
      }
    }

    final report = PlacementDiagnosticReport(
      totalQuestions: _paper.length,
      correctCount: correctCount,
      predictedScore: double.parse(predicted.toStringAsFixed(1)),
      weakestDomain: weakest,
      domainScores: domainCorrect,
    );

    telemetry.logEvent(
      eventName: 'PLACEMENT_TEST_COMPLETED',
      parameters: {
        'predictedScore': report.predictedScore,
        'weakestDomain': report.weakestDomain.name,
        'isPass': report.isPredictedPass,
      },
    );

    setState(() {
      _report = report;
      _isFinished = true;
    });
  }

  Widget _buildOptionTile(int index, String text, QuestionItem q) {
    final optionNumber = index + 1;
    final userSelected = _userAnswers[_currentIndex] ?? [];
    final isSelected = userSelected.contains(optionNumber);

    return GestureDetector(
      onTap: () => _onOptionTapped(index),
      child: AnimatedContainer(
        duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 120),
        margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: isSelected ? AppColors.amber400.withOpacity(0.15) : AppColors.slate900,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
          border: Border.all(
            color: isSelected ? AppColors.amber400 : AppColors.slate800,
            width: isSelected ? 2 : 1,
          ),
        ),
        child: Row(
          children: [
            Container(
              width: 28,
              height: 28,
              alignment: Alignment.center,
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                shape: BoxShape.circle,
                color: isSelected ? AppColors.amber400 : AppColors.slate800,
              ),
              child: Text(
                '$optionNumber',
                style: TextStyle(
                  color: isSelected ? AppColors.slate950 : AppColors.slate400,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                ),
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 14),
            Expanded(
              child: Text(
                text,
                style: TextStyle(
                  color: isSelected ? AppColors.amber400 : AppColors.slate50,
                  fontSize: 15,
                  fontWeight: isSelected ? FontWeight.bold : FontWeight.normal,
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildReportView() {
    if (_report == null) return const SizedBox.shrink();
    final rep = _report!;
    final isPass = rep.isPredictedPass;

    return SingleChildScrollView(
      physics: const BouncingScrollPhysics(),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: AppColors.slate900.withOpacity(0.8),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
              border: Border.all(color: isPass ? AppColors.emerald400 : AppColors.rose500, width: 2),
            ),
            child: Column(
              children: [
                Icon(
                  isPass ? Icons.verified_rounded : Icons.warning_amber_rounded,
                  size: 56,
                  color: isPass ? AppColors.emerald400 : AppColors.rose500,
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                Text(
                  isPass ? '測驗結果：預估及格' : '測驗結果：預估尚未及格',
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 18,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                    color: isPass ? AppColors.emerald400 : AppColors.rose500,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                Text(
                  '${rep.predictedScore}',
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 56,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w900,
                    color: isPass ? AppColors.emerald400 : AppColors.rose500,
                    fontFamily: 'monospace',
                  ),
                ),
                const Text('10 分鐘落點快測預估得分 (及格線 60 分)', style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate400, fontSize: 12)),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 16),
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: AppColors.rose500.withOpacity(0.12),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
              border: Border.all(color: AppColors.rose500.withOpacity(0.4)),
            ),
            child: Row(
              children: [
                const Icon(Icons.vaccines_rounded, color: AppColors.rose500, size: 24),
                const SizedBox(width: 12),
                Expanded(
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      const Text('診斷致命弱點領域：', style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.rose500, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                      const SizedBox(height: 4),
                      Text(
                        rep.weakestDomain.name,
                        style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate50, fontSize: 16, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 2),
                      Text(
                        rep.weakestDomain.description,
                        style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate400, fontSize: 11),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 24),
          ElevatedButton.icon(
            onPressed: () {
              HapticFeedback.heavyImpact();
              Navigator.pop(context);
            },
            icon: const Icon(Icons.flash_on_rounded, color: AppColors.slate950),
            label: const Text('消滅弱點領域死穴', style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate950, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900)),
            style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(backgroundColor: AppColors.amber400, padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16)),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          OutlinedButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
            style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(side: const BorderSide(color: AppColors.slate800), padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 14)),
            child: const Text('返回戰情室', style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate300)),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildQuestionView() {
    if (_paper.isEmpty) return const SizedBox.shrink();
    final q = _paper[_currentIndex];
    final domain = SkillTreeDomainMapper.resolveDomain(q);

    return SingleChildScrollView(
      physics: const BouncingScrollPhysics(),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 4),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: AppColors.blue500.withOpacity(0.15),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
                  border: Border.all(color: AppColors.blue500.withOpacity(0.4)),
                ),
                child: Text(
                  domain.name,
                  style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.blue500, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                ),
              ),
              Text(
                '#${_currentIndex + 1} / ${_paper.length}',
                style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate400, fontFamily: 'monospace', fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 14),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 20),
          Text(
            q.question,
            style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 18, color: AppColors.slate50, height: 1.5, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 28),
          ...List.generate(q.options.length, (idx) => _buildOptionTile(idx, q.options[idx], q)),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (_paper.isEmpty) {
      return Scaffold(
        backgroundColor: AppColors.slate950,
        appBar: AppBar(title: const Text('🎯 10分鐘實力落點快測')),
        body: const Center(
          child: Text('題庫初始化中或無可用考題...', style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate400)),
        ),
      );
    }

    if (_isFinished) {
      return Scaffold(
        backgroundColor: AppColors.slate950,
        appBar: AppBar(
          title: const Text('🎯 診斷報告與落點預測'),
          automaticallyImplyLeading: false,
        ),
        body: SafeArea(child: _buildReportView()),
      );
    }

    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: AppColors.slate950,
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: const Text('🎯 10分鐘實力落點快測'),
        actions: [
          Center(
            child: Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 16.0),
              child: Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 4),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(color: AppColors.slate800, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12)),
                child: Text(
                  '${_currentIndex + 1} / ${_paper.length}',
                  style: const TextStyle(color: AppColors.amber400, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      body: SafeArea(
        child: Column(
          children: [
            LinearProgressIndicator(
              value: (_currentIndex + 1) / _paper.length,
              backgroundColor: AppColors.slate900,
              valueColor: const AlwaysStoppedAnimation(AppColors.amber400),
              minHeight: 4,
            ),
            Expanded(child: _buildQuestionView()),
            Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
              decoration: const BoxDecoration(
                color: AppColors.slate950,
                border: Border(top: BorderSide(color: AppColors.slate800)),
              ),
              child: Row(
                children: [
                  Expanded(
                    flex: 2,
                    child: OutlinedButton(
                      onPressed: _currentIndex > 0 ? () => setState(() => _currentIndex--) : null,
                      style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
                        side: const BorderSide(color: AppColors.slate700),
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 14),
                      ),
                      child: const Text('上一題', style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate300)),
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(width: 12),
                  Expanded(
                    flex: 3,
                    child: _currentIndex < _paper.length - 1
                        ? ElevatedButton(
                            onPressed: () => setState(() => _currentIndex++),
                            style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                              backgroundColor: AppColors.amber400,
                              foregroundColor: AppColors.slate950,
                              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 14),
                            ),
                            child: const Text('下一題', style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                          )
                        : ElevatedButton.icon(
                            onPressed: _submitAndCalculateReport,
                            icon: const Icon(Icons.analytics_rounded, color: AppColors.slate950),
                            label: const Text('交卷並產出落點報告', style: TextStyle(color: AppColors.slate950, fontWeight: FontWeight.w900)),
                            style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                              backgroundColor: AppColors.emerald400,
                              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 14),
                            ),
                          ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _DuoChunkyButton extends StatefulWidget {
  final String text;
  final Color color;
  final Color shadowColor;
  final VoidCallback? onPressed;
  final double height;

  const _DuoChunkyButton({
    required this.text,
    required this.color,
    required this.shadowColor,
    required this.onPressed,
    this.height = 56.0,
  });

  @override
  State<_DuoChunkyButton> createState() => _DuoChunkyButtonState();
}

class _DuoChunkyButtonState extends State<_DuoChunkyButton> {
  bool _isPressed = false;

  void _onTapDown(TapDownDetails details) {
    if (widget.onPressed == null) return;
    HapticFeedback.lightImpact();
    setState(() => _isPressed = true);
  }

  void _onTapUp(TapUpDetails details) {
    if (widget.onPressed == null) return;
    setState(() => _isPressed = false);
    widget.onPressed!();
  }

  void _onTapCancel() => setState(() => _isPressed = false);

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final bool isDisabled = widget.onPressed == null;
    final double pushOffset = _isPressed ? 6.0 : 0.0;
    final bgColor = isDisabled ? AppColors.slate800 : widget.color;
    final sColor = isDisabled ? AppColors.slate900 : widget.shadowColor;
    final fgColor = isDisabled ? AppColors.slate500 : AppColors.slate950;

    return GestureDetector(
      onTapDown: _onTapDown,
      onTapUp: _onTapUp,
      onTapCancel: _onTapCancel,
      child: AnimatedContainer(
        duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 100),
        curve: Curves.easeOut,
        height: widget.height,
        margin: EdgeInsets.only(top: pushOffset, bottom: 6.0 - pushOffset),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: bgColor,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
          boxShadow: [BoxShadow(color: sColor, offset: Offset(0, 6.0 - pushOffset))],
        ),
        alignment: Alignment.center,
        child: Text(
          widget.text,
          style: TextStyle(
            color: fgColor,
            fontSize: 16,
            fontWeight: FontWeight.w900,
            letterSpacing: 1.0,
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
